🗓️ WEEK MAP 09–13/11

🧠 AI 36 — AI VALUE CHAIN MAP

🧠 THE AI 36 — 12-LAYER AI VALUE CHAIN MAP (from the "The Action Plan" slide — Tom sent 09/11, Iggy researched + drew the zones)
Chain logic: no power → no chips → no models → no apps. Capex flows from Layer 9 down to Layer 1; revenue is booked as each bottleneck is cleared: Layers 1–2 benefit EARLIEST (power + infrastructure posting the strongest numbers) · Layers 3–6 collecting "rent" mid-cycle (chips/HBM/optics in short supply) · Layers 7–8 in the middle (revenue bunched into 2027–28) · Layers 9–12 LATE — Layer 12 alone is priced farthest AHEAD, with the highest "right thesis, wrong timing" risk.
Every name carries its BUY ZONE · TARGET · CUT per the current zones.json — zones are technical references, NOT buy/sell signals. Prices = CLOSE 09/11/2026.
🚀 TOP BREAKOUT POTENTIAL (ranked by closeness to the trigger + upside)

💥 FAA 13/11: TRUMP DECLARES 'NATIONAL EMERGENCY' OVER AIR TRAFFIC CONTROL — DUFFY ASKS CONGRESS FOR $50 BILLION
Friday evening 13/11, Homeland Security Secretary Mullin said at a White House press briefing: President Trump has declared the air traffic control failure a 'national emergency'; Transportation Secretary Duffy raised the ask to Congress from $30,1 billion to $50 billion to overhaul the system. Cause: radar/communications instability from 15:36 ET after a patch on legacy technology; no evidence of a cyberattack. Delayed flights have topped 25.000; the nationwide ground stop of more than 4 hours was lifted in the afternoon of 13/11.

💥 IRAN 13/11: ISRAELI ARMY SAYS NO URGENT NEED TO STRIKE IRAN — DE-ESCALATION SIGNAL

⚡ FED 13/11: DALY — 'ALMOST CERTAINLY WILL HAVE TO RAISE RATES', 10Y UP TO 5,17%
San Francisco Fed President Mary Daly today 13/11 (UNLV event): the 'bias' is toward raising rates, 'almost certainly will have to raise rates' — services inflation is moving the wrong way, businesses have no room left to absorb costs. This is Daly's 2nd statement in 2 days, after Collins this morning. The market confirms: the 10Y yield hit a 4-week high of 5,17%, the DXY is at a 1,5-year high, pricing a 100% chance of a 25bp hike at the 8–9/12 FOMC; BofA raised its forecast to 3 consecutive hikes in December + January + March; gold ~$3.879, lowest in 13 months.
• updated 10:42 PT 13/11 · Yahoo Finance qua Reuters — “Fed's Daly sees more rate hikes as firms seek to pass on costs” 🔗
💥 IRAN 13/11: 'NOT ONE PARTICLE OF ENRICHED URANIUM LEAVES' — MOSCOW ALREADY TOLD
Mohsen Rezaei, secretary of Iran's Supreme National Security Council, told Al Mayadeen on 13/11: Iran will not allow even one particle of enriched nuclear material to leave Iran; Tehran has told Moscow it will keep all of it. The statement came the same day a confidential IAEA report warned of losing track of a stockpile of near-weapons-grade enriched uranium — Tehran is both refusing monitoring and locking away the material, narrowing the path for nuclear talks. Related: uranium, Brent/WTI oil, XLE.
• updated 12:32 PT 13/11 · Yahoo Finance qua Reuters / Israel RealTime — “Iran rejects transferring enriched nuclear material abroad” 🔗
💥 IRAN 13/11: IAEA SOUNDS THE ALARM — IRAN REFUSES NUCLEAR MONITORING WHILE UNDER THREAT
On 13/11, a confidential IAEA report sent to member states (seen by both AP and Reuters): Iran says talks on resuming nuclear monitoring cannot proceed while it is under threat of military attack; the IAEA warns it has been unable to verify the stockpile of near-weapons-grade enriched uranium since June and does not know the location/status of the Isfahan fuel enrichment plant — "a widespread issue requiring urgent remedy". Only the Bushehr plant has been inspected since June; the report will be discussed at next week's Board of Governors meeting. Related: uranium, Brent/WTI oil, XLE.
• updated 12:05 PT 13/11 · Yahoo Finance via Associated Press — “Iran Tells UN Nuclear Agency That Monitoring Cannot Proceed While It Is Under Threat” 🔗

📖 MONEY-MAKING GUIDE — read it to survive in the market

📊 How many waves are there in a year? S&P 500 since 1950: on average 3–5 times 5%+ shakeouts, ~1 time 10%+ corrections every year, every 4–7 years a 20%+ bear move. Red is normal — green is the reward.

🗓️ What to do each month: Month 9 the worst in history → hold cash. Month 10 is the month of bottoms (1987, 2008, 2022 all bottomed in month 10) → watch for bottom-fishing, don't fear the red. Months 11→4 the money season → attack. Months 5→8 choppy → be selective, preserve capital.

🎯 When does opportunity come — look at this board: 😨 VIX > 25–30 = panic = when you smell money. 😱 Capitulation (down ≥6% + big volume + big hands catching) = time to accumulate. 🏷️ Discounted goods ≥20% off the 52-week high = brand names on sale. Price back to the Buy Zone only then consider it — no chasing.

💀 Hard-learned lessons: ① No stop-loss — a 10% loss becomes 50%. ② Averaging down a falling knife with no plan. ③ Chasing tops on FOMO — jumping in when you see green = buying someone else's top. ④ All-in in one shot — out of ammo right when the real opportunity arrives. ⑤ Impatience — real opportunities come only a few times a year.

🛡️ Capital preservation: hard cuts, no negotiation • 3 books kept separate, no cross-covering losses • cash is a position too.

🔑 KEY: Patience > intelligence. Capital > profit. Cutting losses is insurance, not failure. You only need to get 2–3 big waves right each year to make a living.

Price zones are technical references only, not buy/sell orders. The decision is each person's own.
